I am debugging why Wio Tracker L1 Pro does not announce  into Bitchat. I suspect it crashes somewhere, usually a stack overflow or a heap overflow. It is in NFC52 bitchat bridge code. There's a lot of debug stuff printed. Here's the full diagnostic prompt from the serial. After this, it stops.

Is the time synced? Did some client connect? Which one? What could have happened after? Could a message have crashed it? Investigate.

When there are repeating patterns (like two lines repeating), replace with [repeats XXX times]. But only if they are unchanged and there's nothing in between.
